# SP41_CW_81 changes and reading decisions

All readings below were adjudicated against the supplied scan images. C and H are independent draft witnesses; S is the supplied sweep. Raw independent reader returns and crop manifests are retained under `readers/`. Macro-only differences are excluded from H collation. Retained C doubt marks are recorded even where no substantive witness change results.

Counts of confidence concern distinct logged change loci, so a locus differing from both witnesses is counted once there. C totals include resolved or changed uncertainty marks as editing changes; they are not an error count. Retained C doubts with no textual change are excluded. H totals count differing primary readings, omissions, placement, drawings and deletion/insertion state; adding or removing a doubt mark alone is excluded.

## Consequential corrections

- Scan 27, Slanted note, first equality: The first positive-parts comparison is a plain equality; H supplies a crossed comparison.
- Scan 27, Slanted note, équivaut à: The margin writes équivaut à before E1=Sigma12; H substitutes implique.
- Scan 27, Slanted note, overmark on Sigma: The marginal Sigma carries a left over-arrow, omitted by H and left circle/arrow-uncertain by C.
- Scan 28, Arrow after S in second incidence formula: The second incidence map carries a visible left-end decoration; its shape remains uncertain, while H prints a plain arrow.
- Scan 28, Index at the second coproduct in X double-prime: The final coproduct index is retained as c|a, with c first from its open curve; its summand remains E_a.
- Scan 28, Lowest index in first E of the propriety inequalities: The lowest index in the first properness inequality is retained as b|0, with b first from its short stem; other a_0 occurrences stay as written.
- Scan 29, relation between A prime and the underlined Gamma: The relation after A-prime is retained as membership|inclusion, with the internal return stroke placing membership first.
- Scan 29, word before parallèles in the opening paragraph: The opening reads bien parallèles; the closing non parallèles is also retained as written.
- Scan 29, kind of topological object: The topological object is called arbre; H reads ensemble.
- Scan 29, object whose arêtes are considered in NB: The live NB object is script X-star, with a possible overwritten underlined Gamma retained explicitly; H gives underlined-Gamma through its bold encoding.
- Scan 30, Words H places between d’ens and the nonempty sign: The short insertion contains only d’ens ≠∅; H’s extra disjointness words are absent from the ink.
- Scan 30, H’s extra triple-star label between the underlined word and the parenthesis, line 16: H’s extra (***) after ceci is absent; the separate lower (***) label remains in its written place.
- Scan 30, Inserted incidence clause and the cancelled preceding formulation at the foot: The cancelled equality and the live incident-edge replacement retain their distinct written states and attachment.
- Scan 30, Final displayed E subscript: The final displayed E subscript is retained as c|a from its open curve; the earlier E-family still has written index a.
- Scan 32, Superscript on Sigma in (4): Formula (4) preserves the written omega exponent. H’s displayed epsilon and S’s proposed epsilon are recorded separately from the ink; H itself acknowledges omega in its note.
- Scan 33, (6), relation following omega: The joined relation in (6) is retained as membership/non-equality/equality uncertain, with membership first; H’s clean equality does not settle the ink.
- Scan 33, (7), second left exponent: high prime and low old suffix: The separate high prime is preserved; the two entries in (7)’s parenthesis are both visibly primed, while the first live epsilon in the union expression is unprimed. No consistency repair is inserted.
- Scan 33, (7), first epsilon in the right parenthesis: The separate high prime is preserved; the two entries in (7)’s parenthesis are both visibly primed, while the first live epsilon in the union expression is unprimed. No consistency repair is inserted.
- Scan 34, (13), first Sigma in the definition of E_2: The first factor in the written E2 definition is Sigma1-plus. H and S propose Sigma2-plus; the written 1 is preserved.
- Scan 34, (15), right-hand Sigma subscript on first row: The union term in (15) carries the written low index 22. The expected 12 is recorded as a witness proposal, without replacing the two curved digits.
- Scan 34, (15), relation immediately below the written (13): The relation beneath the reference (13) has three bars; preserve equivalence rather than H’s two-bar equality.
- Scan 35, Right-hand live Pi in the equality after alors: The source writes Pi1=Pi3 after displaying the Pi1 and Pi2 pairs. Its two-turn 3 is preserved; H prints 2.
- Scan 35, End of the proposition line mutuellement non ...: The proposition reads non panachées. H substitutes non parallèles; the visible p/h/é letter sequence is retained.
- Scan 35, Ordered pair, first Sigma superscript: The first ordered-pair superscript is T-shaped. Preserve top as first reading with plus retained, without supplying an upper stroke.
- Scan 36, First comparison in (***) a): The first relation retains a visibly crossed sign as ne first, with equality and final revision state still open. S’s proposed equality is not substituted.
- Scan 36, First lower index on the right of (****): The extra low 2-shaped remnant after the live 1 is represented, with its cancellation state unresolved; H gives only 1.
- Scan 36, (***) a), relation between E1 and Sigma12: The second a) relation also retains ne first with equality and final revision state open; its exact witness strings are recorded separately from the first sign.
- Scan 37, Slanted note, fourth Sigma lower index: The written primary form is retained from its observed strokes; no replacement based on the surrounding mathematical argument is supplied.
